## Source ownership and evidence levels
The supplied @vortex_math screenshot reproduces Kobayashi–Nitta Figure 4 (arXiv:1304.6021 v2 PDF p4), including panel labels, green n3=0 and blue n1=-0.97 isosurfaces. Original source https://arxiv.org/pdf/1304.6021 ; first submission 2013-04-22 https://arxiv.org/abs/1304.6021 ; DOI 10.1016/j.physletb.2013.12.002. PDF pp4–5 visually inspected. The authors solve a classical extended Faddeev–Skyrme model numerically; no quark detection. Personal priority investigation retired by user direction; original scientific and independent Rodin/ABHA source records remain in scope.

### Earlier primary mathematical and physical owners
- Gladikowski and Hellmund, 1996-09-03, https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/9609035 : O(3) Hopf solitons, numerical charges 1,2; cites Faddeev's 1975 proposal and 1979 Vakulenko–Kapitanskii bound. Not a quark model.
- Faddeev and Niemi, 1996-10-24, https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/9610193 ; Nature 1997 DOI 10.1038/387058a0 : numerical indications of unknot/trefoil solitons and conjecture of all torus knots. https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/9705176 (1997-05-22) investigates toroidal unknot.
- Battye and Sutcliffe, 1998-11-30, DOI 10.1103/PhysRevLett.81.4798 : numerical knots/links through Hopf charge 8; standard FS trefoil charge 7.
- Aratyn, Ferreira and Zimerman, 1999-05-11, https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/9905079 ; DOI 10.1103/PhysRevLett.83.1723 : exact toroidal static solitons in a DIFFERENT integrable theory, two integer labels whose product is Hopf charge. https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/9902141 earlier unit-charge solution.
- Hietarinta and Salo, 1999, DOI 10.1016/S0370-2693(99)00054-4 : linked unknot relaxation depends on relative handedness/direction.
- Finkelstein, 2004-08-27, https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/0408218 ; Finkelstein and Cadavid 2005-07-01 https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-th/0507022 : speculative quantum-group trefoil model of fermions, explicitly quarks and leptons. Not empirically established.
- Bilson-Thompson, 2005-03-22, https://arxiv.org/abs/hep-ph/0503213 : B3 framed preon braid candidate for quarks, leptons and gauge bosons; not a confirmed substructure.
- Sutcliffe, 2007-05-10, https://arxiv.org/abs/0705.1468 : rational maps S3 -> CP1 supply initial torus-knot configurations and numerical FS solutions up to Hopf charge 16. Not the later domain-wall kink construction.
- Kobayashi and Nitta, 2013-04-17, https://arxiv.org/abs/1304.4737 : quadratic-potential toroidal domain walls with P,Q in 1,2,3, C=PQ. 2013-04-22 paper adds linear potential, distinguishes kink curves and stability: (3,2) stable in their numerical search, (2,3) and (2,2) not found; not a universal nonexistence proof.

### Independent Rodin / ABHA lineage
- Russell P Blake, https://www.rexresearch.com/rodin/6-blakemathformulation.pdf : explicit attempt at modulo-nine formalization, hosted copy associated with 2007 project filename, exact original publication date not established.
- Marko Rodin and Greg Volk, 2010 NPA proceedings, https://www.rexresearch.com/rodin/3-numbermapcoil.pdf ; p6 Figure 2 and Table 9 visually inspected. Explicit twisted periodic map, three circuits connected by column shift not divisible by three, opposite current directions, 396693 gap pattern and recommended 12 poloidal / 5 toroidal turns. This is actual discrete and winding formalization, not the same FS action, not a quark model; text search no quark. Conclusion acknowledges absence of controlled quantitative studies at publication.
- Randy Powell 2010-04-14 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7pvuTZ5u6Kg : dated public ABHA presentation; 2012-11-08 https://redice.tv/red-ice-radio/vortex-based-mathematics-and-the-abha-torus : public interview. 
- Russell Gries https://www.scribd.com/document/54220752/Russell-Gries-s-Perspective-on-the-Activation-Sequence-of-the-ABHA-Torus-Using-Electricity : 9x9 grid, three 120-degree phase sequences; hosting timestamp not proof of original creation date.
- 2010-12-28 visualization https://www.galacticresonance.org/36x36-abha-torus-firing-sequence-this-is-the/ ; 2012 Wikimedia video https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Web_3D_ABHA_Torus_-_UPDATED.webm .
- Powell 2010-09-26 attributed text preserved in https://thunderbolts.info/wp/forum/phpBB3/viewtopic.php?start=90&t=493 : secondary repost, not independently authenticated original.
- Priority: ABHA public before 2013 verified; mathematical Hopfion product and toroidal solitons precede those ABHA sources. Specific claimant pre-2003 formalization and influence on the physicists not evidenced. Preserve each owner's distinct contribution; do not promote a resemblance to plagiarism or a physical prediction.

### P0A.1 — executed normalized Hopf/Whitehead source (partial D1)

New executable `scripts/check_hopf369_source.py`, commit 7d6c29e92c3af6c5a1eb680a1954f832f5992789, blob 3aaa7a11a31a716fb9c7626fb318161ccdb0a2f0. Local SymPy 1.14.0: 22/22 exact checks PASS; committed Git blob matches the locally executed bytes. Reproduce with `python scripts/check_hopf369_source.py`. Classical Hopf map and Whitehead integral are established prior art; DASHI contribution is exact source-specific cross-consumer construction and discrimination.

Set D=1+x²+y²+z², q=(a,b,c,d)=(2x,2y,2z,D−2)/D. This inverse stereographic map extends to S³, q(infinity)=(0,0,0,1). The Hopf map n=(2(ac+bd),2(bc−ad),a²+b²−c²−d²) satisfies |q|²=|n|²=1. For A=a db−b da+c dd−d dc, using the Euclidean metric in the stereographic chart,

A=2/D² (2xz−2y,2x+2yz,1−x²−y²+z²), B=curl A=4A/D.

For the outward unit-sphere area form ω (integral 4π), n*ω=−2 dA (all three coefficients checked). Thus Ω=ω/(4π) has primitive −A/(2π). Q=∫A∧dA/(4π²)=1 because A·B=16/D³; mirror pullback reverses Q to −1. |A|²=4/D², |B|²=64/D⁴, div B=0. Algebraic identities and radial integral executed; smooth compactification follows from the stereographic construction, but no machine-checked atlas, regular-value linking computation or AFZ/KN equation residual is claimed. In particular Q alone cannot recover the ordered windings/framing. D1 zero-charge field and signed (P,Q) source comparisons remain active.

### F1 — quantitative Fourier convergence

Use orthogonal Fourier modes on side 2a periodic cell, physical k=πn/a, spectral cutoff P_K on |k|≤K, normalized consistently so Parseval is the physical L² norm. For smooth mean-zero B_h, integers m≤s, and K>0:
|| (1−P_K)B_h ||_{dot H^m} ≤ K^{m−s} ||B_h||_{dot H^s}.
Since K (inverse curl; denote operator by Cinv to distinguish cutoff K) commutes with P_K and orthogonal bands, helicity difference is exactly the signed tail sum, not unsigned:

BD1. Reverse-solve regular parallel-normal closure rather than merely add Taylor order. In 5D g=dn²+h, A=h^-1 K, tau=tr A, S=A-tau I/4, s=tr S². E_nn=0 gives tau_n=4-tau²/4-s for Lambda=-6. On the time-reflection fixed slice the adapted normal development has no time-space blocks, so s>=0 by self-adjointness of spatial block; this relies on time reflection and regular chart. Away from that slice Lorentzian s may be negative; explicit mixed-only self-adjoint tracefree witness has tr A²=-2. Equal Lambda and pure tension with tr mean M=0 gives tau(0)=2j, tau(ell)=-2j, j=-6/5. Original AK3 comparator tau0=4 tanh(n-log2) solves shear-free equation and reaches endpoints at L0=2 log2, b=(4/5)cosh(n-log2) exact locally AdS5. Delta=tau0-tau satisfies Delta_n=-(tau0+tau)Delta/4+s, Delta(0)=0; integrating factor gives strict positive Delta(L0) for any positive shear interval. Any regular connecting solution must stay -4<tau<4: comparison at lower barrier -4 precludes return above it, comparison from initial data precludes hitting upper barrier 4. Hence d atanh(tau/4)/dn=1-s/(4-tau²/4) and ell=L0+integral s/(4-tau²/4) dn. In general curvature scale c, ell>=2/c atanh(-j/(2c)), with strictness if positive shear, assuming -2c<j<0. Equivalently at fixed proper normal length ell, |j|<=2c tanh(c ell/2), strict under nonzero shear. For time-reflection-invariant regular global parallel-normal chart with constant ell and original coordinate fibre length L0 (g_yy=1 at t=0), every curve between the cut walls on the spatial slice has length >=ell, contradicting a fibre of length L0 if any ray has shear. These are conditional no-go results, not a proof of global normal chart existence or nonexistence of general periodic geometry; coordinate y period alone is not Gaussian normal length, nonparallel terminal wall/caustics or changing radion invalidate the fixed-length assumptions. Do not replace original signed data to evade the obstruction without declaring the changed physical input.

BK1. Exact normal Sturm-Liouville owner from AK5/BJ: Nq=-(b^3 q')'+4b^3 q on cut [0,L], common endpoint q, [q']=j q with same-oriented difference q'(0)-q'(L); b(0)=b(L)=1 and [b']=j. Write q=b z, then z,z' periodic, N(bz)-z Nb=-(b^5 z')'/b, Nb=3h0² b². Integrate on original domain to obtain <q,Nq>=int b^5|z'|²+3h0² int b|q|², including j|q_wall|² in original form. Hyperbolic radial indicial Laplacian h0² p(p-2) for delta^p, so first homogeneous exponents -1 and 3, all other decaying indicial exponents >=3. This is a statement for sufficiently regular asymptotic coefficients, not yet a polyhomogeneous regularity theorem for every solution in weighted Hölder spaces.

BK2. Stronger actual AN divergence cancellation. Set t=|x|², delta=1-t, F=2(1+t)/h0, D=delta^4+F²t. In original coordinate a=delta a_reg, write a_reg=A(t)x3 x+B(t)e3+C(t)(e3 cross x), with A=2eps(2F³delta/D²+(4delta/h0+4F)/D), B=2eps F delta(delta^4-F²t)/D² and C=4eps F² delta³/D². Toroidal term divergence zero. Hyperbolic divergence div_gamma a=(h0² delta³/4)x3 V(t), V=4A+2t A'+2B'. Exact SymPy rational reduction:
V=32eps h0 delta(t+1)(4h0²delta²+t²+6t+1)/(h0²delta^4+4t(1+t)²)².
Thus div_gamma a=O(delta^4) with all normalized fixed-order derivatives, not only O(delta³). Leading BF seed perturbation in hyperbolic frame is mixed a plus tangential f j (sym nabla_gamma a)^TF; horizontal diagonal/even terms O(delta^4), higher odd C0 terms O(delta^6). For this leading tracefree perturbation on AK, DR=div div h (Ric=-3g). Put d=div_gamma a, Bwarp=b'/b; direct warped covariant divergence gives (div h)_i=b^-2 f j div_gamma(sym nabla a)^TF_i+3 Bwarp a_i and (div h)_y=b^-2 d. For a regular trial w=delta³ q3+O(delta^4), leading T/delta³ =6(q3_yy+3Bwarp q3_y-4q3+3h0² q3/b²); it vanishes iff q3=b times arbitrary angular amplitude. BL1. Use actual BK m=2 projected trial, with [pinned projected normal coefficients](../Artifacts/hopf369/bl_projected_coefficients_20261126.json) (36 constant-normal coefficients and all zero channels retained; blob 0904aa9fbeb50ccff30cd9e37c44d1246c64d959, commit 72454d447fc1a1aab3094b773e293e4e2e12a5d2) and [original BG 252 coefficients](../Artifacts/hopf369/bg_endpoint_constraint_receipt_20261126.txt). Projection coefficient quadrature only chooses a trial; exact mathematical input is stored binary64 numbers regarded as rationals with exact basis constants. The actual trial is not an exact nonlinear solution. Let g0=AK spatial metric, gs=G0+(u-1)G1, g=u² gs, D=u³ G1. With eta a reciprocal variation and v=u eta, delta g=-(2g+D)v, so L v := -DT(w)[v/u]=DR_g((2g+D)v)=-6 Delta_g v-2 R_g v+DR_g(Dv). Original reciprocal seam [nu_seed eta]=j eta and [nu_seed u]=j(u²-u) imply [nu_g v]=jv exactly; common value retained. D and first derivatives vanish on both seam faces by BE f=f'=0.

BL2. Exact weak-form identity. All raising/contraction in the following definitions uses g unless stated. C=Gamma(g)-Gamma(g0), W^{ij}=2g^{ij}+D^{ij}, T^k=W^{ij} C^k_ij-W^{ik}C^l_il, V1^k=nabla^k tr_g D - nabla_i D^{ik}, M^{ij}=(6+tr_g D)g^{ij}-D^{ij}. Ric_g=Ric0+nabla_k C^k_ij-nabla_j C^k_ik-C^k_kl C^l_ij+C^k_jl C^l_ik (derivative here is actual g connection), Ric0=-3g0. Set
Vbase=3 W^{ij}g0_ij + (nabla_k D^{ij}) C^k_ij-(nabla_j D^{ij}) C^k_ik+W^{ij}(C^k_kl C^l_ij-C^k_jl C^l_ik).
At seam with same oriented nu_g, integration by parts of -W Ric v² yields boundary coefficient [nu_g·T]. Because D=0, actual Christoffel jump contributes -12j to nu_g·T, and reference Christoffel jump contracted with actual g contributes -6j(alpha+1/alpha)/u_wall. Thus [nu_g·T]=6j(-2+(alpha+1/alpha)/u_wall). Define m(x)=-1+(alpha+1/alpha)/u_wall, Z=Bwarp sqrt(g^yy) m partial_y, Bwarp=b'/b. Then [nu_g·Z]=j m, so total physical boundary coefficient 6j+[nu_g·T]=6jm is exactly canceled by adding divergence of 6 Z v², no boundary residual. Put ell=V1+2T-12Z and V=Vbase-6 div_g Z. Pointwise strong/weak identity v L v = M(dv,dv)+v ell·dv+V v²+div_g U, U=-v M(dv)-v²(V1+T-6Z). Its two seam fluxes sum to zero for v satisfying Robin; compact support in base or H1 cutoff limit supplies the integral identity. Bounded coefficients give a bounded weak bilinear form on matching-trace H1, with natural Robin condition in the weak equation. The diagonal form, obtained by density of smooth transmission functions or by the corresponding weak integration by parts, is the displayed bulk quadratic form. No symmetry assumed. At reference D=C=0, Z=Bwarp partial_y, M=6g0^-1, V=24-6(1+2Bwarp²), ell=-12Z; Schur margin 18h0²/b²>=11.52. [Actual weak evaluator and independent regression](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bl.py), commit 0cb3b30aeac30b3b0af54e95a90417bb06f70b85 blob edce17f4049131c84b188b03e16f7ee756d1607b. BL3. Exact source norm certificate (not sampling). In gamma-orthonormal frame a=epsilon h0 delta² [F delta I+(4delta/h0+4F)xx^T] W/Dden², t=|x|², delta=1-t, F=2(1+t)/h0, Dden=delta^4+F²t>0 on closed ball, W=(delta^4-F²t)e+2F²(e·x)x+2F delta² e cross x with unit distinguished axis e=e3. This is linear in e and SO(3)-covariant jointly in x,e; covariant derivatives in isotropic gamma preserve those properties. For fixed radius squared norm is a quadratic form in e invariant under rotations fixing x, hence eigen-directions parallel and orthogonal to x exhaust the angular extrema (also at origin by isotropy). In frame e_i=h0 delta/2 partial_i, Gamma^k_ij=h0(x_j delta_ki-x_k delta_ij), differentiate a polynomial numerator N/Dden^p by total partial_i+2x_i partial_t and include connection contraction on each covariant index, increasing denominator power by one. Restrict to pole/equator only AFTER differentiating. Exact rational polynomial tests target² Dden^(2p)-sum N² positive at both ends and zero roots on [0,1] by SymPy exact root counting (degrees 32,48,64). All six tests pass: A=13/250=.052 for ||a||, B=29/200=.145 for ||nabla_gamma a||_F, C2=49/100=.49 for ||nabla_gamma² a||_F. Valid for |epsilon|<=1/40 by homogeneity. Reuse BK exact divergence formula, maximum angular |x3| at pole, same rational root exclusion proves divA=13/100=.13 for |div_gamma a|. [Source certificate](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bl_source.py), commit ce272684dae9f4ceeb8b420f7a229adbdcc70b31 blob ddcbbb8a04d5dba63c54aeaf7dbad6000ae370d3. All fixed target assertions pass, no reliance on sampled estimates. BI B=.91 replaced by much tighter bound.

BL4. Actual projected reciprocal trial first derivative envelope over 512 dyadic t intervals and 256 dyadic normalized y/L intervals (131072 cells covering all r and y, angular bounds exact). From BJ use rational outward Taylor enclosures of b,b', pi,L, trig and endpoint polynomials (no floating transcendental interval claim). IEEE binary64 interval arithmetic for basic ops and sqrt widened with nextafter after every operation, exact binary64 coefficients, Fraction constants enclosed at conversion; assumes correctly rounded basic operations/sqrt, no fastmath, finite outputs; local ordinary IEEE numpy environment. Write Fpref=delta²/(1+10t), tau=1-t², cA=Fpref tau², cB=Fpref tau t². For each angular channel H_i, coefficient W_i=cA(f0 P_i+Q_i)+cB f0 Pstar_i, radial argument s=t/(.1+t)=1-.1/(.1+t) with s_t=.1/(.1+t)². ## BN — explicit-amplitude global scalar constraint by ordered barriers (26 November 2026)

BN0. Reconciled #1554 at 5c1d20189c88c9cb5652f7083254e2479a5d729c, Lean #122 at 64db5f277b5cdbf979527797699b91fc8b4289ca, #1291 at c68b44f37c89b54d9ba0b5ef18ab4753c7dd5f04 (actual indexed roadmap blob 3da5e97aec36b019c7d0742bf53f2323aa6e3fc3; H15 is finite signed-strip geometry, not this PDE), F8 #1527 at d19a9525968ec2fee92a6e3f73027df05396d497. Reopened AN metric ffdd57a98eea54be991801468e9e20816b56ab0d, BF coupled scalar f6df412a6637b5b70a399c5acbe58f9cd4882717, BJ compactification 7349e33ef6112fbe500d54afc51c064b5886268c, BL weak form edce17f4049131c84b188b03e16f7ee756d1607b and BM actual trial. Reuse BI ellipticity and BE exact junction, not the BM small C1 tube as an existence hypothesis. Direct execution; no native subagents/ITIR proof service available. No unrelated Clay edits.

BN1. On the original coupled family let delta=1-|x|², alpha=(1+|a|_gamma²)^(-1/2), f0=1+(b-1)alpha, w_±=±(2/25)f0 delta². Both obey [nu_seed w]=j w exactly, because f0 has equal value 1 and jump j; the correction tensor has vanishing value and first derivative at the seam. The scalar T(w)=R(g(w))+12 is evaluated on the actual w-dependent tensor, not a frozen seed. BI gives positivity and ellipticity for |w|<=.1 and here |w_±|<=.08. For the zero-source reference w_b=t b delta², exact conformal algebra yields T_b=-18h0² t delta²(1+delta)/b+h0² t² delta⁴(-54+30delta). This is the reference subtraction, not an approximation of the nonzero source.

BN2. Exact regular enclosure: set g_b=(1+t b delta²)^(-2)g_AK, g=g_b+delta² E. Use f0-b=(1-b)delta⁴|ahat|²/[sqrt(S)(sqrt(S)+1)], a_norm=delta² ahat, and u-u_b=-u u_b delta² t(f0-b); thus E=-(u+u_b)u u_b t(f0-b)g_AK+u² A0, A0=(g_seed(u)-g_AK)/delta² from BF/BJ. All operations are regular at the conformal boundary. Compute the connection difference as delta² C using the reference conformal connection, with C obtained by raising the symmetrized derivative of E plus 2 dlog(delta) times E. The scaled Ricci difference is Q. Exact quotient T/(f0 delta²)=T_b/(f0 delta²)+(tr_g Q-tr(g^-1 E g_b^-1 Ric(g_b)))/f0. The first term is evaluated analytically. See [interval jet evaluator](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bn.py), commit 448c73a7c2dd0195ade71fc0359605445cacc355, blob f7d3cebe94a2b88deccc9935af4a90336514388f.

BN3. Full angular reduction: original numerator W=(delta⁴-F²r²)e+2F²(e·x)x+2F delta² e×x, F=2(1+r²)/h0, D=delta⁴+F²r², ahat=epsilon h0[F delta W+(4delta/h0+4F)x(x·W)]/D². Proper rotations take x to r e1 and source axis to (c,s,0). Axis reversal is epsilon reversal, equivalent to y->L-y for this metric and barrier; therefore c,s>=0 suffice, including the equator and pole. Parameterize c=(1-p²)/(1+p²), s=2p/(1+p²), p∈[0,1]. Differentiate ordinary Cartesian jets before restriction to the radial axis. Linearity in e gives 120 exact rational functions (two axis components, three tensor components, twenty Cartesian multiindices through order three); coefficients are Taylor coefficients divided by multiindex factorial. Exact numerator/denominator gcd and Bernstein conversion in common degree, followed by dyadic de Casteljau subdivision, certify all denominator Bernstein coefficients positive and bound each ratio by min/max of the coefficient ratios. See [radial certificate](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bn_radial.py), commit eb850e326c40cd1e70fda79659145eda31cfb52c, blob a3b77ac795efc2ab4af7f1f635c20b8b1197a4bd.

L=2 log 2 enclosed by rational atanh series with explicit remainder; b and b' by rational Taylor remainder; monotone angular endpoints; all subsequent elementary interval operations outward rounded via nextafter in binary64 (correct rounding, no fastmath, no flush-to-zero assumption). BN5. [Independent replay](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bn_verify.py), commit 6f11279cf33e8765b3a74f210da6b17d4356000e, blob b4937a1a99c8dc9cb7a069b11786ede9c1629186. No CI or native kernel.

BN6. Convert the seam into an ordinary scalar quasilinear problem: z=w/f0. For piecewise C1 functions with equal traces, [nu_seed w]=j w iff [z_y]=0, since [nu_seed f0]=j, tangential derivatives match, and the common normal coefficient of partial_y is positive. z_±=±.08 delta² are smooth on M=H3×S1_L. On this carrier use the smooth reference gamma+dy². The principal part of -T(f0 z) is -A^{ij}(x,z)nabla_ref_ij z, A=f0 P(x,f0 z), with P the BI positive principal tensor. A is symmetric, uniformly elliptic on |z|<=.08, locally Lipschitz jointly in x,z across the glued seam: at the two endpoints f0=1, G1=0, g and P agree for each fixed z. Lower terms C are piecewise smooth with at most quadratic growth in dz (metric algebraic in z, curvature affine in Hessian and quadratic in first derivatives). Write -div_ref(A dz)+B=0, B=C+(nabla_ref_i A^{ij})_(z fixed) z_j+(partial_z A^{ij})z_i z_j. There is no delta source from A because its traces agree. B is Carathéodory and satisfies natural growth |B|<=K(1+|dz|²) on the slab in uniform reference charts. Clip the z argument of both A and B outside the slab, preserving ellipticity and growth (the extension outside the slab need not be curvature).

BN7. On each exhaustion |x|<R<1, identify the periodic domain with the smooth bounded solid torus in R4 via ((2+x1)cos(2pi y/L),(2+x1)sin(2pi y/L),x2,x3). Absorb the positive smooth reference volume and coordinate transforms into divergence coefficients. The ordered z_± satisfy the weak sub/supersolution inequalities for -T by BN4, with matching flux (no seam source), and bracket exterior Dirichlet datum zero. Apply Boccardo, Murat, Puel, Résultats d'existence pour certains problèmes elliptiques quasilinéaires, Ann. Scuola Norm. Sup. Pisa 11 (1984) 213–235, Theorem 2.1 p217, assumptions pp213–214, https://www.numdam.org/item/ASNSP_1984_4_11_2_213_0/ : p=2, uniform coercivity, strict gradient monotonicity of A(x,z)p, Carathéodory coefficients, bounded z on slab, natural quadratic growth and ordered bounded H1 barriers. This produces a weak H1_0∩L∞ solution z_R on each solid torus between the barriers; does not assume BM monotonicity or impose an artificial seam boundary.

BN8. Local regularity and passage to complete carrier. On reference chart balls freeze A_R(x)=A(x,z_R(x)); ellipticity and growth constants uniform on fixed compact charts independent of R. Testing with cutoff² phi(z_R), phi(s)=s exp(K s²) for sufficiently large K so phi'-(growth/ellipticity)|phi|>0, gives local H1 bounds by Young. Exponentials exp(±k z_R), k>=growth/ellipticity, are weak subsolutions of -div(A_R d·)<=constant. Apply classical weak Harnack to the nonnegative supersolutions exp(k M)-exp(k z_R), exp(-k m)-exp(-k z_R) for local essential M,m. Their values comparable to M-z_R,z_R-m, sum comparable to oscillation; weak Harnack on smaller balls gives osc shrink by theta<1 plus O(radius²), hence uniform local C0,alpha. See N. S. Trudinger, On Harnack type inequalities and their application to quasilinear elliptic equations, Comm. Pure Appl. Math. 20 (1967), 721–747, DOI 10.1002/cpa.3160200406. With A_R Hölder, compare z_R on ball B_r with harmonic replacement h for constant A_R(center), same trace. Test z_R-h, use max principle for h and osc bound, yielding int|d(z_R-h)|² <= C r^alpha (E(r)+r^4), E(r)=int|dz_R|². Constant-coefficient energy decay and iteration give E(r)<=Cr^(4-epsilon) for epsilon=alpha/2. Gradient Campanato oscillation Phi(r)=int|dz_R-(dz_R)_B_r|² satisfies Phi(rho)<=C(rho/r)^6 Phi(r)+Cr^(4+alpha/2); iteration yields C1,beta for some beta>0 (e.g. beta=alpha/8). All on glued smooth charts, including seam where A is continuous; B only needs measurable x. Local C1 compactness and exhaustion diagonal subsequence pass the weak equation via dominated convergence on compact charts, preserving barriers. This is an existence argument, not a numerical solve or assertion of uniqueness on the entire slab.

BN9. One-sided C2 regularity: A(x,z(x)) is locally Lipschitz once z is C1,beta; B bounded, local difference-quotient W2,2 for divergence equation. Nondivergence rewrite A^{ij}partial_ij z=F a.e. with F piecewise C0,beta up to seam. In a flattened local chart let H=[F]/A^yy|seam. Extend the one-sided normal jet phi=0,dphi=0,phi_yy=H at seam, others zero, e.g. phi=(y²/2)(rho_|y|*H)(x_tan) on positive side with cutoff, zero negative. Hölder mollifier estimates give piecewise C2,beta and globally C1. Then F-A:D²phi is globally Hölder. Linear Schauder gives z-phi locally C2,beta: one may solve linear Dirichlet on a small ball with the same trace and Hölder coefficients/forcing, compare via W2,2 weak divergence rewrite and absorb bounded drift using Poincaré on a sufficiently small ball. Hence z piecewise C2,beta, common first jet. w=f0 z satisfies exact reciprocal seam. Scalar curvature -12 holds on both sides up to seam. BE gives J2_TF=0 exactly and [R]=-2tr J2 implies full J2=0. At the selected epsilon=±1/40 this gives actual classical one-sided spatial initial data, time-symmetric vacuum constraints, fixed tension spatial Israel junction; use BE log-lapse Hermite construction on this wall. No full spacetime/corner solution follows.

BN10. Asymptotic class: barriers give z=O(delta²). On fixed bounded-geometry reference charts delta comparable to center delta0; rescale z=delta0² Z. BK gives T(0)=O(delta⁴) with normalized derivatives. The scaled natural-growth term bounded by C(delta⁴/delta0²+|Z|+|dZ|+delta0²|dZ|²), with uniform normalized coefficient bounds (BE/BK); the BN8/9 estimates apply uniformly to Z, giving piecewise normalized derivatives through order two O(delta²). Average linearization L=-int_0^1 DT(sw)ds, Lw=T(0). Its coefficients tend to AK at infinity including zeroth coefficient since w and derivatives decay. BK reference comparison functions psi_p=f0 delta^p, p=2,5/2 have strict positive limiting Lpsi_p/psi_p, hence strict positivity outside some compact core. Choose C so Cpsi_5/2 dominates |w| on inner boundary and L(Cpsi_5/2)>=|T(0)| throughout exterior. Apply maximum principle to (±w-Cpsi_5/2)/psi_2 (bounded, matching first jets after division); exhaustion -log delta has bounded normalized derivatives, and one-sided Hessians at seam maxima obey the same sign. Conclude w=O(delta^5/2), and repeat scaling local regularity for weighted piecewise C2,beta. No evaluated tail constant, no delta³ expansion or analytic regularity asserted. Exact g_yy=u² and g_iy/g_yy=a_i; proper fibre length between L/(1+.08delta²) and L/(1-.08delta²), tends L, not Gaussian-normal separation. Negative epsilon solution by reflection, epsilon=0 exact u=1. BN existence is independent of BM narrow C1 neighborhood: neither membership of BN solution in that tube nor global uniqueness proved.

BO1. Time-reflection corner in spacelike Gaussian normal coordinate n and wall proper time t. Write spatial H, k±=M±j H/2, D±=k_n, F±=k_nn, m=tr_H M, s=tr((H^-1 M)^2). BN supplies common D. Timelike h=diag(-1,H), mean timelike tensor has M_tt=m, M_ti=0, spatial M; hence full mean trace zero. BC normal Einstein RHS with Lambda=-6 gives
H_tt=2[D-Ric(H)-(4-j²/2)H-2MH^-1M].
Both sides demand the same six-component acceleration. BC third-normal spatial jump requires [F]=j(4+s+m²)H-12jMH^-1M. Spatial scalar identity R_g=R_H-2tr_H D+3tr((H^-1 k)^2)-(tr_H k)^2, derivative [partial_n R_H]=-2j R_H by constant-scale variation, and R_g=-12 on each side imply tr_H[F]=j(12-9s+3m²). Thus exactly five independent third-normal conditions remain:
E3=[F]^TF+12j(MH^-1M)^TF=0.
This condition is necessary for a sufficiently differentiable, time-reflection symmetric spacetime with the stated fixed-tension junction; not a separate thin-shell law or proof of failure of weak lower-regularity evolution. [Symbolic checker](../scripts/check_hopf369_p0bo.py) commit e8c8026bb872839e711524e8c5d9579796d2ff1b blob 5df3f4435c9abc236e9c3532a5b28c5e6e82758d, arbitrary full symmetric M,D in orthonormal frame, exact rational nonorthogonal change, all acceleration components and third trace/TF identities.

BO2. Exact conformal-sector rigidity and signed first-order control. At epsilon=0, g=exp(2phi)(dy²+b² gamma), phi=-log(1+w), physical H=exp(2phi_wall)gamma and k± are pure trace, [phi_y]=j(exp(phi_wall)-1). Put X=(Hess_gamma phi-dphi⊗dphi)^TF. Intrinsic Ric(H)^TF=-X, spatial Ric(g)_tan^TF=-2X, hence D^TF=X. Conformal Ricci mixed jump [Ric_yi]=-2d_i[phi_y]+2j d_i phi+2[phi_y]d_i phi=0. In Gaussian coordinates the derivative of the tangent frame at the seam adds only terms proportional to this mixed jump; [partial_n Ric(g)_tan]^TF=exp(-phi)[partial_y Ric(g)_tan]^TF=-2j X. Differentiating the spatial D equation gives [F]^TF=-[partial_n Ric(g)_tan]^TF+j D^TF=3j X=-3ju (Hess_gamma w_wall)^TF. Hence E3=0 implies pure-trace wall Hessian in this sector. Divergence yields d(Delta_gamma w-3h0² w)=0; for decaying w and derivatives the constant vanishes, maximum principle forces w_wall=0. This is a rigidity diagnostic, not a claim that the nonzero-source BN solution fails its condition. For first-order epsilon perturbation of AN/BE at AK: mixed metric a, tangential correction j f (sym nabla_gamma a)^TF, no yy variation from source. In Gaussian gauge the tangential coordinate vector is -I(y)a^sharp with I'=b^-2 and I=0 at each face. Its induced tangential metric correction is -2b² I sym nabla_gamma a, plus j f TF term. f''' agrees at both faces and (b² I)'''|face=4b''/b=4, so source third jump cancels to first order. On unique smooth small BE branch reflection implies first-order w odd under y->L-y, w_wall first variation zero. Consequently E3=O(epsilon²) on that branch in sufficiently regular norms. No evaluated bound at .025; BN branch membership not proved.

BO3. BN regularity upgrade as a local analytic argument using BN9 linear transmission estimate: once z=w/f0 is piecewise C2,beta with common first jets, take tangential difference quotients of its nondivergence equation. The differentiated principal coefficient is the same continuous elliptic A; forcing from smooth one-sided coefficients and lower derivatives is piecewise Hölder with uniform local quotient bounds. Difference quotients have matching value and first normal derivative; apply BN9 jump removal and local Schauder estimate to gain two derivatives for each tangential derivative. Solve the original equation for z_yy (A^yy>0), differentiating that expression on each side to recover higher pure normal derivatives. Induction gives piecewise smooth regularity on compact wall charts (and smooth interior by ordinary elliptic bootstrap), conditional on the same classical transmission estimates used in BN. This is not analytic regularity or an evaluated uniform analytic radius. It licenses the following formal higher-jet analysis for the BN data.

BO4. Exact cubic tensor repair at a fixed sufficiently regular spatial solution. Let seed wall H_seed, S=g_seed^yy|wall>0, physical H=u_wall² H_seed. Choose p3(y)=y³(L-y)³/(6L³), with jets through order two zero at both ends, p3'''(0)=1, p3'''(L)=-1, reflection even. Add p3 C3(x) to the horizontal tangential seed block before multiplying by u², with
C3=-u_wall S^(-3/2) E3.
The physical coordinate third metric derivative changes by ±u_wall² C3, and no lower derivatives change. Normal geodesic charts with the same initial wall and normal agree through cubic order: connection perturbation is O(n²), geodesic position difference O(n⁴). Thus the third tangential Gaussian metric derivative changes by ±u_wall² (sqrt(S)/u_wall)³ C3; F=H_nnn/2, so jump changes by S^(3/2)C3/u_wall=-E3. Exactly E3_new=0; C3 is trace-free in seed and physical wall metrics. In the actual sheared metric, g^ij-g^iy g^jy/g^yy=H_phys^-1, so the principal coefficient in the scalar normal derivative on a pure y³ tangential correction contracts only its H_phys trace; the one-sided R and its first normal derivative remain unchanged at the wall. Hence at fixed w both the scalar trace of [F] and all previous lower jets are preserved. KK reconstruction g_yy=u² and g_iy/g_yy=a_i is unchanged pointwise, including radion at this fixed trial. Signed reflection transfers the repair (E3 is even under simultaneous side and normal reversal), zero comparator has zero correction. Positivity is local for a sufficiently thin supported collar; no assertion global polynomial p3 stays positive or solves the bulk scalar equation.

BO5. Derivative-loss-free reduced corner residual and extension. On actual family T(w)=P^{ab}w_ab+B(x,y,w,dw), P depends on w, not dw. At seam both P traces coincide, P^yy=6S/u>0, and reciprocal first seam implies [w_y]=j w_wall/sqrt(S). Scalar jump equality gives
[w_yy]=-(2P^{iy}partial_i(j w_wall/sqrt(S))+[B])/P^yy,
where B is the lower-order remainder, evaluated on each side. The highest third derivatives in F are linear with coefficients depending only on common metric (normal-geodesic formula, or differentiated curvature formula). At seam g_w=-2u g. Therefore pure y³ third scalar-unknown derivatives contribute only a trace to F; no w_yyy dependence remains in E3. Pure tangential third derivatives cancel in the jump, the two-tangent one-y jump follows from differentiating [w_y], and the one-tangent two-y jump follows from differentiating the displayed [w_yy] expression. All involve at most second jets. Thus on scalar-jump-compatible functions E3 is a smooth function of the two one-sided second jets and fixed source jets. For off-equation iteration define its reduced extension by imposing the displayed jump in these expressions (retain the mean normal second derivative). This makes the proposed nonlocal third-correction map C^{2,beta} -> C^{0,beta} on the wall, rather than demanding a third derivative of the unknown. In H^m, m>=5, the second-jet trace is H^{m-5/2}; smooth products locally valid there (m-5/2>3/2). Use independent endpoint extensions with signed cubic normal profile y³ exp(-y Lambda)/6 times cutoff, Lambda=(1-Delta_H3)^(1/2) acting on tensor components in bounded-geometry charts (or compatible tensor elliptic spectral operator). The elementary spectral integral norm scales lambda^(2k-7) for k normal derivatives; a third-jet lift H^{m-7/2}->H^m follows, and the reduced residual has one derivative of spare trace regularity. Alternatively use local mollified y³/6 times wall C3 with smoothing scale |y| and cutoff at d, patch in charts, and corresponding reversed y at opposite end. This gives C^{3,beta} lift with prescribed third jet, and C^{2,beta} norm O(d^(1-beta)) times the C^{0,beta} wall norm (by sup D²=O(d), bounded D³, Hölder interpolation); use bounded geometry and comparable local weights for a global weighted version. A frozen such lift can have arbitrarily small C² effect while paying the exact third jet. The BK/BL/BN original barrier certificate is for the original family; cannot declare it a certificate of the nonlocal modified family. New global fixed-point/degree or inverse estimate must control the feedback and the weighted tail, including positivity, admissibility, and actual same-solution attachment.

BO6. Local scalar repair and formal continuation. With analytic wall w and one-sided w_y data, freeze C3 from BO4, insert p3 C3 in BE and solve the scalar equation in normal coordinate y by noncharacteristic analytic CK (P^yy>0 at seam). Same w,w_y data give identical w_yy by the scalar equation at seam, identical w_yyy by its differentiated equation because the cubic trace-free correction leaves T_y unchanged. Thus the corrected spatial metric is locally constraint-satisfying and keeps the repaired third jet, original wall, first two jets and connection. Radion away from the wall changes under this scalar re-solve; do not assert it stays identical there. BN currently has smooth rather than proven analytic traces, and the construction is local, not a replacement global solution at epsilon=.025. Without analyticity the scalar recursion determines compatible formal normal coefficients (Borel extension only gives a smooth function with residual flat at the seam, not exact PDE existence).

BO7. Replace BA fixed-shape ansatz by full prescribed even-in-t determinant-one wall shape. Write h=-dt²+H, H=exp(2p) Hhat(t,x), det Hhat=det H0, A=Hhat^-1 Hhat_t/2, tr A=0, P=H^-1 H_t/2=p_t I+A, theta=tr P. M_tt=sigma, M_ti=B_i, Msp=H(Q+sigma I/3), where Q is H-self-adjoint trace-free, allowed to vary in t via conjugation from an H0-self-adjoint trace-free path. Exact Gauss scalar R_h=R_H+2theta_t+theta²+tr(P²)=R_H+6p_tt+12p_t²+tr(A²). Exact Codazzi gives sigma_t=div_H B-4p_t sigma-tr(AQ), B_t=div_H Msp-3p_t B. Normal Gauss imposes
6p_tt=-12+3j²-4sigma²/3-tr(Q²)+2|B|_H²-R_H-12p_t²-tr(A²).
These are BB's original principal blocks in (p_t,d p,sigma,B), with prescribed smooth Hhat,Q and added lower-order shape terms. For a prescribed analytic shape and Q, intrinsic CK and BC normal CK give local analytic wall/bulk development under the original hypotheses, not smooth sideways well-posedness. Time-even Hhat,Q,p,sigma and odd B retain the original parity. At the corner choose p=p_t=0, B=0, sigma=m, Q0=H0^-1 M-m I/3. Choose Hhat_tt to be the TF part of BO1 required H_tt; p_tt trace is precisely the intrinsic Gauss equation by the spatial scalar identity. At fixed these jets, choose the TF second time derivative of Q (in an H0-frame before conjugation) to match the mean spatial F^TF. In BC derivative of Ric(h) along 2K the highest time derivative is K_sp,tt with coefficient one, while sigma_tt and B_t are fixed by lower jets and chosen Hhat_tt. Thus this mean-F control is invertible on five components. When BO4 repairs [F]^TF, both sides can match the actual prescribed third spatial jets; traces are forced by spatial scalar differentiation. No numerical BN wall representative available or computed here.

BO8. Formal all-order corner geometry (not convergence). In the wall proper-time frame, twice principal intrinsic Ricci variation at pure time covector acts on symmetric 4x4 C as S(C)_sp=C_sp, S(C)_ti=0, S(C)_tt=-tr_H C_sp, so S²=S. In BC recurrence h_nn=2 Ric(h)+lower terms, highest pure-time spatial derivatives at even normal order 2r are partial_t^(2r)H with coefficient identity; at odd order 2r+1 they are 2 partial_t^(2r)K_sp, all lower temporal order contributions or already chosen even H derivatives retained. Formal Gauss/Codazzi solve scalar/trace and mixed time jets; the free TF shape time jet at even order and free TF mean time jet at odd order can be chosen recursively to match one side's full spatial series (trace fixed by scalar constraint, formal Bianchi propagation and time symmetry). The opposite side is then determined by the same wall data and fixed tension. Its next highest spatial jet can be changed through the source-preserving horizontal metric block: a coordinate y^k tangential coefficient with all lower jets zero contributes u²(nu^y)^k times that coefficient to Gaussian tangential k-jet; this is invertible. Trace-free part supplies five independent geometric coordinates, and trace supplied by scalar recursion or horizontal trace correction. This describes a formal recursion for a compatible modified spatial series and identifies all-order matching tests for a fixed original series. Does NOT prove that the original BN global metric passes them, that the modification converges, or that its scalar residual vanishes throughout the bulk. In particular formal matching cannot be substituted for hyperbolic physical-time existence, boundary estimates or AdS continuation. BP1. Frozen smallness with a uniform weighted supremum cap rather than a circular derivative bound. Let z=w/f0, delta=1-|x|², reference bounded-geometry cut charts on H3 x S1. ## BQ–BR — fourth-order compatibility, joint saturated feedback and conformal harmonic transmission (27 November 2026)

BQ0. Reconciled #1554 predecessor d2455d60cad4a26a4b211e202a300c3c36c1a536, BP1–6/BR1–2 actual equations; #1291 c68b44f37c89b54d9ba0b5ef18ab4753c7dd5f04, #1508 5abb227a3c5733c243c7e2eabcba36e671b3e28a, #1512 52357be06c1ff086ad4c1c16d51852bcec122fec, #1527 d19a9525968ec2fee92a6e3f73027df05396d497. New classical donor Andersson–Oliynyk, A transmission problem for quasi-linear wave equations, JDE 256 (2014) 2023–2078, DOI 10.1016/j.jde.2013.12.014, https://arxiv.org/abs/1401.0277, Theorem 1.1, Remark 1.2, sections 3–4 and section 5 first-order derivative variables for moving material coordinates. It concerns fixed interfaces and jumps in lower-order terms, not a delta-stress timelike shell. Extracted theorem/assumptions inspected; PDF screenshot retrieval was blocked by host. No Codex-native workers/ITIR proof tools exposed. Direct analytic implementation and separate bounded symbolic tests.

BQ1. Fourth normal jet: use BC h_n=2K, D=K_n, F=D_n, G=F_n; write mixed A=h^-1 K, B=h^-1 D, X=h^-1 F, U=h^-1 M, C=h^-1 mean(F), q=tr U². At wall A±=U±jI/2, tr U=0, tau±=±2j, [D]=0, [X]=j((4+q)I-12U²), tau_n=4-q-j², tr B=4+q+j². From E_nn=0, tau_nn=-2tr AB+4tr A³ and tr X=4tr AB-4tr A³. First derivative of normal Einstein RHS yields X=h^-1 DRic_h(2K)+L(A,B), L=(8-tau_n)A-tau B+2(BA+AB-2A³). Since Ric(h) as covariant tensor is constant under constant positive scale, DRic_h(h)=0 and D²Ric_h(h,M)=-DRic_h(M). Therefore [Ric(h)_nn]=-8j DRic_h(M), and h^-1 DRic_h(M)=(C-mean L)/2, using actual mean third jet. Twice differentiating RHS gives
h^-1 G=h^-1 Ric(h)_nn+8B-tau_nn A-2tau_n B-tau X+2(XA+AX+2B²-4BA²-4A²B+8A⁴-2ABA).
Eliminate Ric variation with mean L and expand:
h^-1[G]=j[(40+6j²-2q)U-12(BU+UB)-4C+(2tr UB-4tr U³)I].
This is a full four-dimensional timelike tensor jump, not an imposed fourth-order field. Derivation uses earlier corner conditions and full vacuum normal constraint; do not apply to arbitrary unconstrained metric. The mean third jet is retained rather than averaged away. At time-reflection corner h=diag(-1,H), U_tt(mixed)=-m, U_sp=H^-1 M_sp, m=tr U_sp. Btt(mixed)=4+q+j²-tr B_sp; Xtt(mixed)=4tr AB-4tr A³-tr X_sp on each side, mixed time-space entries zero by parity. Thus all inputs are known from actual first, second and third spatial jets. Signed reflection U,C odd, B even implies [G] odd; zero AK has U=C=0 and [G]=0.

BQ2. Independent spatial trace identity. Write m=tr M_sp, s=tr M_sp², t3=tr M_sp³, b=tr D_sp, d=tr M_sp D_sp, c=tr mean F_sp, all mixed with H^-1. Spatial scalar R_g=R_H-2tr B_sp+3tr A_sp²-tau_sp². Second derivative terms besides -2tr G_sp are R_H,nn+14tr A_sp X_sp-76tr A_sp² B_sp+10tr B_sp²+72tr A_sp⁴-2(tau_sp,n²+tau_sp tau_sp,nn). Scalar R_H scaling degree -1 implies D²R_H(H,M)=-2 DR_H(M); mean first differentiated constraint gives DR_H(M)=c-5d+6t3+m(b-2s). Thus [R_H,nn]=-16j DR_H(M). Substitution of [F_sp] from BO1 gives
tr_H[G_sp]=j(6bm-4c-18d+4m³-8ms+16m-12t3),
exactly the spatial trace of BQ1. Hence five independent fourth-order TF conditions. This calculation independently checks the trace rather than assuming it from a formal spacetime already solved.

BQ3. Fourth repair and reduced residual. Define E4=([G_sp]-G_target,sp)^TF from BQ1. Use a fourth-jet lift with coordinate endpoint fourth derivatives +C4 and -C4, jets through three zero, C4=-u_wall² S^-2 E4. Change of spatial Gaussian fourth tangential derivative is +/-u²(nu^y)^4 C4 and G=H_nnnn/2, so jump changes by -E4. Geodesic chart variation begins at fifth order, no lower jet change. KK connection and fixed coordinate period preserved by horizontal block correction, radion unchanged at fixed trial (may change after scalar solve). Pure fourth tangential-block metric derivative enters second normal derivative of scalar curvature via the same Schur trace as BO4, so its TF correction leaves R and its first two normal derivatives at wall unchanged. Fourth residual can be evaluated using at most third scalar jets: highest fourth derivatives enter linearly with common metric coefficients; pure tangential fourth jump zero, mixed jumps obtained from tangential derivatives of [w_y] and the original scalar-jump equation and its first normal derivative; pure fourth y-derivative of scalar is conformal at wall and removed by TF. In each differentiated scalar jump equation the coefficient of the highest pure normal derivative is P^yy>0, other principal terms carry already reduced jumps, and products with differentiated coefficients have at most one lower total order. Include actual cubic lift: for an even tangential mollifier and cutoff constant near wall its fourth pure normal jet vanishes and the order-four mixed jets depend on one tangential derivative of C3 (hence at most third scalar jet). Terms involving lower jets are retained. This is an order-four specialization of BO5, not an all-order derivative-loss theorem. Define off-equation extension by enforcing the reduced scalar jumps in highest-jet expressions, use BQ1 target with lower actual means even before third correction is exact; only fixed point is interpreted geometrically.

BQ4. Joint cubic/quartic feedback existence argument, conditional on BP/BN transmission estimates and BQ3 reduced-residual algebra. Use separate fixed collar widths d3,d4 and saturation caps M3,M4. General local y^r/r! lift of bounded continuous tensor, mollification scale y, smooth cutoff constant near 0, opposite signs adjusted for parity, has weighted C^{r-1,alpha} norm <= C M_r d_r^(1-alpha), jets below r zero, exact r-jet trace. If coefficient C^{p,beta}, lift has piecewise C^{p+r,beta}, by same convolution estimates/Taylor cancellation as BP1, constants may depend on fixed width. Choose allowable combined weighted C2,alpha0 norm first to retain BI positivity and half BN barrier margin; allocate half to each lift, and bound quartic lift in weighted C3,alpha0 by a sufficiently small allowance independent of M4. Uniform C2,beta bound for any slab fixed point follows exactly BP3–5 independent of caps/widths. Hence bound actual C3 sup and choose M3 then d3 (independent M4,d4). With d3 fixed, C2,beta gives cubic coefficient C0,beta, so cubic lift bounded C3,beta with constants depending on d3; quartic lift already bounded C3,alpha0. Differentiate the scalar equation tangentially once and use common principal coefficients, BN9 linear transmission estimate and pure-normal recovery to get uniform local weighted C3,beta' bound independent of M4,d4. Consequently E4 reduced coefficient bounded independently of M4,d4, choose M4 then d4. This breaks the circular bound; not a computed radius.

BQ5. Upgrade BP4's actual compact fixed-point map to third-order topology, avoiding nonsmooth clamp derivative. On each bounded periodic exhaustion use smooth normalized value saturation c(v)=delta² s(v/delta²), s identity on [-.08,.08], nondecreasing, with .08<=s(r)<=min(r,.08+eta) for r>=.08 (odd reflection for negative), constant outside slightly larger interval. Choose eta>0 small enough to retain BI slab and at least e.g. one quarter original BN strict signs even when coefficients are evaluated at c(v) within eta delta² of the barrier but barrier derivatives are unchanged; curvature operator is uniformly smooth in scalar value and relevant metric jets, this changes residual by O(eta delta²), including at noncompact tail. At fixed point on {z>z+}, compare linear principal and truncated gradient with upper barrier at this frozen scalar value; strict upper residual sign persists, z-c(z)>=0. Maximum principle (bounded drift from truncated gradient) forces no positive excess. Lower reflection analogous. Thus c(z)=z on actual solution; smooth map need not use a nonsmooth exact clamp off solution. For trial data with compact-support feedback define capped C3,C4, with C4 evaluating reduced residual including the capped cubic lift (off-equation extension only); fixed caps ensure C2/3 allowances. Use BP4 same linear Dirichlet map, with successive C0,gamma, C1,gamma, local piecewise C2,gamma and C3,gamma bounds on nested compact interiors covering support. Third-order bound uses input C2,gamma to control C3 lift in C3,gamma, fixed quartic allowance in C3,alpha0, smooth c and gradient saturation, differentiated tangential forcing piecewise C1,gamma; BN9 differentiated and normal recovery. Choose exponents sufficiently small (finite steps), then compactness in global C1 and local piecewise C3 with these bounds, continuity via uniform convergence on support and linear estimates/compact embeddings. As in BP5, barrier gives C2, then C3 estimates on interior independent of artificial outer boundary; both caps inactive by BQ4. Exhaustion diagonal compactness and locality of lifts produce a joint fixed point with original scalar and both true repairs, assuming the stated BN/BP estimates. ## BR14–BR17 full warped reference reduced symbol, bulk forcing and initial harmonic gauge (27 November 2026)

Reuse #1554 predecessor 1ec233b0f090d43ff9137aeb80cd188ee44ee1d0, BR1/9/10 continuous prescribed harmonic-source moving interface, BR12 optical scalar transfer, BR-A homogeneous gauge propagation, AY4/BE5/BQ conditional spatial jets and BR4 cusp gauge. BR14. Full warped reference symbol in the *continuous prescribed lower harmonic source* gauge of BR1/9. In AK use proper n, harmonic Y with dn=b^4 dY, optical x with dx=dn/b, and base Riemann normal coordinates at a tangential point (eta=diag(-1,1,1,1,1), index n last). Prescribe H_cov equal to reference contracted lower Christoffel in these coordinates, independent of perturbed metric. Let p be metric perturbation normalized by b^2 for tangent-tangent, b^5 for tangent-Y, b^8 for YY, so at the reference point these are orthonormal component values. All 15 independent symmetric components retained. Away from wall linearized reduced Einstein E_red=Ric+4g-nabla_(mu C_nu); on reference E_red=0,C=0, delta C=div p-d tr p/2+A(p). In coordinate expression A_mu=p_mu,nu Gamma^nu-p^ab Gamma_mu,ab (all background contractions), with Gamma^nu the contracted Christoffel. In normalized components at the base RNC point A=k P(p), k=b_n/b, P_a=-2p_na, P_n=tr_eta_t p_tt-4p_nn. The rough tensor wave uses infinitesimal covector connection L_a v: (L_a v)_n=-v_a, (L_a v)_a=eta_aa v_n, other entries zero, and tensor representation Lt_a p=L_a p+p L_a^T. Write I_a(v)=e_a v^T+v e_a^T. All component maps are literal 15x15 matrices, not separate copies of scalar wave. The optical normal derivative block is R=3I+I_n P. Tangential derivative matrices J_a=2 eta_aa Lt_a+I_a P. Zeroth warp matrix W=sum_a(eta_aa Lt_a^2+I_a L_a P), curvature matrix C0:p->2(p-eta tr_eta p), normal derivative matrix Vn=R-3I. Base RNC connection-jet term Vbase is the sum of (number of tangential indices)*p and sum_a I_a A_base,a, where (A_base,a)_i=(8/3)p_ia-(2/3)eta_ia tr_eta_t p_tt for tangent i, (A_base,a)_n=2p_na; multiply by base sectional curvature -h0². These coefficients follow from h_ab=eta_ab-K[(x.x)eta_ab-x_a x_b]/3+O(|x|³), K=-h0². In particular -sum eta^aa partial_a Gamma^d_ab=K delta^d_b. On freezing tangential coordinate jets at this point (derivatives -> zeta=(s,i xi), not asserting translation symmetry of AdS4), the exact all-component warped normal ODE is
 p_xx + bk R p_x - ell² p + bk sum_a J_a zeta_a p + [b²(W+C0)+h0²(-W+Vn-Vbase)]p=0,
 ell²=s²+|xi|². Derivation uses -2 delta E_red=Box p+2 Riemann(p)+2 nabla_(mu A_nu), with Riemann(p)=p-g tr p in local AdS5. In particular this is not the earlier flat fifteen-scalar comparison. Exact independent coordinate linearization from metric Taylor jets, inverse, Christoffel, Ricci and lower contracted harmonic source (not this frame derivation) passed all 15 components at two controls b=4/5,k=0 and b=1,k=3/5, arbitrary nonzero mixed/trace input vector and nontrivial normal and tangential jets. The generic matrix formula is derived analytically, two exact selected direct controls are not exhaustive continuum validation.

BR15. Exact matrix Liouville and glancing estimate. R has eigenvalues -5,1,3, with polynomial spectral projectors ranks 1,4,10, all complete and signed coordinates retained. Let E=b^(R/2), u=E p, then
 u_xx-ell² u + E[bk sum_a J_a zeta_a + b² Vb+h0² Vh]E^-1 u=0,
 Vb=W+C0-R/2-R²/4, Vh=-W+Vn-Vbase+R²/4.
 Here (log b)_x=b_n, (log b)_xx=b². On 4/5<=b<=1 the exact rational infinity-norm bounds from projectors give cond(E)<=64, norm(potential)<=c1|zeta|+c0 with c1=1536,c0=30832/25 (deliberately conservative). For ell=alpha+i beta, gamma=Re s>0, alpha>=gamma, alpha beta=gamma Im s. Exact 2alpha²|ell|²-gamma²|zeta|²=alpha²(2alpha²-gamma²)+gamma² beta²>=0. Thus |zeta|/(alpha|ell|)<=sqrt(2)/gamma, including grazing. BR12 flat optical Dirichlet Green norm yields contraction rho<=6(2c1/gamma+c0/gamma²) and endpoint derivative error <=e_g |ell| max endpoint amplitude, e_g=18(2c1/gamma+c0/gamma²)/(1-rho). At gamma>=10^7, rho<=.001843200074, e_g<=.005539811203. Large damping is an explicit sufficient bound, not an optimal threshold, and all values are in reference dimensionless units. No division by a vanishing normal frequency: zero parameter remains BR8 for its scalar owner; full undamped tensor resonances not discarded or covered by this bound.

BR16. Actual moving-wall seam in new coordinates, at symmetric AK reference. At endpoints b=1, common m=mean u, d=[u]=-f J0 (J0=2j eta+6j nn), coordinate derivative equals u_x+k(D-R/2)u, D diagonal component exponents 2,5,8. From BR9 exact derivative jump, obtain [u_x,ab]=3j m_ab/2+j m_nn eta_ab, [u_x,an]=-5j m_an/2-6j zeta_a f, [u_x,nn]=3j m_nn/2+j tr_t m. The BR9 wall equation converts to ell² f+mean(u_x,nn)/2+(4+3j²/2)f=G with homogeneous value jump residual; this is the transformed form of earlier coordinate coefficient 4+11j², not a contradiction or deletion of terms. With nonzero value residual a, d=-fJ0+a and include the corresponding linear known conormal and wall forcing (BR9 and exact coordinate conversion). For X=max(|ell| ||m||_inf, |ell|²|f|), leading diagonal transfer is -2ell tanh(ell Lc/2)m and ell² f; its scaled inverse norm <=1 (|tanh|>=3/4). Nonleading seam rows norm <= (11|j|/2+6|j|)/gamma+2e_g(1+4|j|/gamma) <.011082; wall row <=2|j|(4/3)/gamma+(4+3j²/2)/gamma²+e_g(1+4|j|/gamma)/2 <.002771 at gamma>=10^7. All 15 metric unknowns and embedding are reconstructed on optical interval, not a separate scalar or decoupled metric approximation.

Bulk forcing addition (analytical): for u_xx-ell²u+V(x,zeta)u=F, zero-Dirichlet forced solve by the same Green contraction, in L² normal using infinity norm componentwise (L² of component max). Schur/Young gives ||G||<=6/(alpha|ell|), ||d_x G||<=6/alpha, endpoint derivative functional <=3/sqrt(2alpha). Thus ||u_F||_2<=6||F||_2/[alpha|ell|(1-rho)], ||u_F'||_2<=6||F||_2/[alpha(1-rho)], endpoint work forcing <=3||F||_2/[sqrt(2alpha)(1-rho)]. For nonzero Dirichlet endpoints flat interpolant and derivative norms <=6 max endpoints/sqrt(2alpha) and 6 |ell| max endpoints/sqrt(2alpha); sum geometric corrections yields the corresponding controlled interior and derivative norms. Feed zero-boundary forced derivative residual into the same 16-channel inverse, preserving separately prescribed continuity residual, conormal residual and wall force. These are anisotropic normal Sobolev/resolvent estimates with fixed tangential coefficients, not a global tangential Sobolev estimate nor a microlocal estimate for perturbed principal metric. In particular cannot absorb high-frequency perturbations of second-order tangential principal coefficients using this lower-order contraction. BR17. Initial harmonic gauge and full acceleration VALUE compatibility in the separate BR4 cusp gauge. Consume original time-symmetric spatial constraint solution (conditional BQ existence and AY/BE first two spatial wall jets), spatial Gaussian wall coordinates n, common H, k±=M±jH/2, D=[one-sided partial_n k values equal], m=tr_H M. Set common positive lapse N, one-sided N_n/N=ell±j/2, ell=-m as BE5. At t=0, shift and all first time metric derivatives zero. Spatial Einstein acceleration A_ij=2N(Hess_gamma N-N(Ric_gamma+4gamma)). AY4 tangential jump vanishes from J2=0 and ell=-m. Mixed jump of Hess is D_a(jN)-j D_aN=0, mixed Ricci jump zero by Codazzi constant j. Normal Ricci jump [Ric_nn]=-tr[J2]+[tr k²]=2jm; choose [N_nn]=2jm N to make normal acceleration common. Equivalently [partial_nn log N]=4jm. Lapse jets are free at this order: prescribe common log N value and independent first/second one-sided derivatives, converted to a fixed cut coordinate by chain rule, use quintic Hermite extension with 6 endpoint constraints (determinant 4L^9 nonzero), exponentiate for positive lapse. Can localize in a collar instead of imposing this polynomial globally. This extends BE5 first-order lapse weld without changing spatial metric or source. For g00=-N²,g0i=0,g_t=0 at t=0, contracted lower harmonic Gamma_0=0, Gamma_i=Gamma_i(gamma)-d_i log N. Set prescribed initial H_i to this, H_0=0. Spatial contracted jump under BR4 J_sp=2j gamma is -2j n_i in dimension four, hence [H_i]=-3j n_i as required. Realize it using BR4 algebraic source H(x,g)=H^common(x)-3j s(y)lambda(g)dy; H^common initialized by adding back the known jump term, common trace, piecewise smooth. Set H_i even in t and H_0 odd; choose partial_t H_0=-tr_gamma A/2 common so N_tt=0, while g0i,tt=0 by parity. Then all 15 initial metric acceleration values agree across the seam (and V transformed via BR4 also has common acceleration values). Given reduced equation at initial slice, C=0 implies E_ij=0 there and the original Hamiltonian/momentum constraints force E_00=E_0i=0 by trace reversal, so partial_t C=0 there as well. This does not assume a reduced solution already exists for positive time; it constructs consistent initial jets and gauge source at the stated order. Initial wall mean equation paid by ell=-m and C=0. Exact local symbolic checks for trace and all normal/mixed/tangent acceleration jumps and Hermite rank. Remaining compatibility: [partial_y g_tt] and higher generated time jets in actual reduced/free-wall chart, source derivative bounds in weighted norm, differentiating embedding equation and its reciprocal work, actual nonlinear local solution; no claim BR4 fixed-interface theorem automatically pays the separate wall law or full moving-interface problem.

## BR18–BR20 arbitrary moving seam, exact gauge chart jet transport and differentiated physical-time corner (27 November 2026)

Reopened #1554 head 950ce878ea0c88966d5a99a393b9ba0692e5406f and actual BO1/BO7/BO8/BQ1–5/BR4/BR17. Reuse the conditional BQ spatial construction, in particular the physical BO third-normal jump; do not build another scalar/collar repair. F8 and indexed geometry donors already pinned in BR12–17. Targeted same-branch reconciliation, not exhaustive cross-PR search in this tranche.

BR18. Exact arbitrary-background material seam linearization in BR1 continuous prescribed lower harmonic source. At a common adapted wall point g=diag(h,1), n_cov=dy, graph y=epsilon f(x), Eulerian metric variation p, define common material metric q=p_±+f g_{y,±}, material derivative r_±=p_{y,±}+f g_{yy,±}. Let z=q_nn, B=mean g_y, D=[g_yy], J=2jg+6j nn. Then [p]=-f J, q=mean p+f B. The graph defining function F=y-eps f has lambda_F=(g^-1(dF,dF))^-1/2 and normal covector lambda_F dF. Exact derivative matching on graph is [partial_y g]=lambda_F(2jg+6j n n); this follows from differentiated metric continuity [partial_a g]=-eps f_a[partial_y g]. Its variation is
 [p_y]+f D=2j q+j z g+9j z nn-6j(n tensor d_t f+d_t f tensor n),
 where d_t denotes all tangential wall coordinates including time, not a pure time derivative. Thus BR9 is only the symmetric B=0,D=0 reference specialization; on actual nonzero mean wall the terms fB and fD must be retained. There are no new derivative orders in this seam relation, but coefficients are actual background jets.

Exact graph wall identity for BR-A alpha=1 is kappa+C_n=lambda_F g^munu partial_munu F + n(log lambda_F)-n^mu H_mu. It is an ordinary defining-function identity (contracted Christoffel cancels), not a new gauge driver. For continuous prescribed H independent of perturbation, at adapted point let W^a=h^{ab}(q_bn+f_b), L_a=partial_a log lambda on background (common tangential trace), H_y denotes the normal component of H, and H_{y,y} its coordinate derivative. Linearized mean reduced wall equation is
 -h^ab f_ab+mean r_nn/2-3 z B_nn/4-W^a(B_an+L_a)+z H_y/2+W^a H_a-f mean partial_y H_y=G.
 In particular the base normal equation mean(H_y)=B_nn/2 changes the z coefficient to -B_nn/2. Derivation: delta n^mu=-z n^mu/2-W^mu, delta d_mu log lambda_F=(material partial_mu p)_nn/2-z partial_mu g_nn/2-W^a partial_mu g_an, plus the f-shift evaluation included in the material derivative. In the wall equation only the mu=y derivative of lambda varies at leading order; f_ab term is the graph Hessian. BR9 recovered at AK where B=0 and H_y=0, with mean r_nn=mean p_y,nn+f mean g_yy,nn; g_yy=b^8 in harmonic Y gives g_yy,YY/2=4+11j² at endpoints. For metric-dependent H add its algebraic variation; do not drop it. These are exact general linearized boundary coefficients; no actual nonzero BQ representative or variable-coefficient bulk parametrix computed. The material field q can be extended into a collar using the graph displacement, but the resulting bulk good-unknown equation and its tame estimates still require control. No assertion the full reference Green inverse directly applies to the perturbed principal geometry.

BR19. Exact same-object chart-jet BIDI between BR1 and BR4. In a fixed-wall sheared coordinate chart n_cov=lambda dy (lambda>0), use a piecewise smooth coordinate transition old x=x(new z) with common identity first jet on wall and only second-jet jump [partial_yy x]=a. Metric derivative jump changes by dy tensor(g a)+(g a)tensor dy. Choosing a=-3j lambda^3 g^-1 dy changes BR1 [g_y]=lambda(2jg+6j nn) to BR4 [g_y]=2j lambda g. The inhomogeneous lower contracted Christoffel/source transformation adds g a g^yy=-3j n_cov at the seam. Prescribe transformed H by pullback of H plus the same coordinate Hessian contraction, so C=Gamma_lower-H transforms as a covector. Inverse transition has opposite second jet (at common identity first jet), recovers original jump and source. Exact arbitrary sheared Lorentz metric calculation includes shift and lapse. Local piecewise coordinate realization by quadratic collar with cutoff and shrinking width, common first jet, invertible near wall. In the adapted lambda=1 chart this is simply [old y,new yy]=-3j. For a fixed transition the transformed source is algebraic in g and smooth on each cut face. For a moving/solution-dependent transition its coefficients can involve second derivatives of the wall/coordinate map; therefore this exact jet-level BIDI is not a uniform PDE equivalence/tame estimate for the free-boundary unknown. Do not assume the BR1 reference inverse proves BR4 evolution or revive BR7 derivative gauge. Physical metric, source and signed jump preserved; no changed KK geometry inferred from coordinate readout.

BR20. Differentiated physical-time corner repair, beyond BR17 value compatibility. Work initially in spatial Gaussian n at t=0 with H, k±=M±jH/2, common D=k_n, m=tr_H M, q=tr_H M² (mixed contraction), bD=tr_H D. Use BO1 exact third-normal spatial jump [F]=j(4+q+m²)H-12j M H^-1 M. For initial lapse N>0, N_n±=N(-m±j/2), define E=Hess_gamma N-N(Ric_gamma+4gamma), A=2N E the physical spatial acceleration in zero shift velocity chart. BR17 paid [E]=0. Set a0=4+q+3j²/4-bD and choose N_nn±=N(a0±jm), a refinement of BR17 lapse jet. This makes E_nn=0 individually. Differentiate actual spatial Ricci tangential formula Ric_ab=Ric_H-D+2kH^-1k-tau k; [partial_n Ric_H]=0 by constant conformal scale, and use BO1 [F]. Differentiated Hess_ab uses [partial_n Gamma_H]=0, [N_n]=jN and [N_nn]=2jmN. Exact residual ([E_ab,n]-j E_ab)/N=j(mean(N_nn)/N-a0)H. Thus [A_ab,n]=2j A_ab with the selected a0. No new physical TF jet needed: BO1 is precisely the donor. Normal component [A_nn,n]=0 by choosing [N_nnn]=N(j a0-2jm²+[partial_n Ric_nn]); independent third lapse derivatives. Prescribe value and first three derivatives of log N at both faces by degree-seven Hermite extension (8 endpoint constraints, determinant 144 L^16), or supported collars, and exponentiate. Positive N, original spatial metric unchanged. Convert derivatives via chain rule if cut coordinate differs from Gaussian.

Important correction to BR17: mixed E_na=-(M+m I)dN-N div_H M is generally not zero (same BT static obstruction). In Gaussian chart [partial_n Ric_na]=-4j Ric_na, by differentiating Codazzi and using [partial_n Gamma_H]=0. Differentiated Hess_na jump is 3j(d(Nm)+M dN). Consequently [E_na,n]=-3j E_na, [A_na,n]=-2j A_na, NOT the desired j A_na before conformal coordinate adjustment. Thus merely increasing lapse jet order with zero initial shift velocity does not close this corner. Repair by wall-fixing spatial coordinate acceleration x_old=x_new+t² v/2 with v and first spatial jets zero at seam, v^n identically zero in local collar, and [partial_nn v_a]=3j A_na. Such v from signed quadratic collar, no physical initial metric/K_time change. New g0i,t=gamma_iv (nonzero off wall), acceleration A_new=A+L_v gamma; its mixed derivative jump increases by 3j A_na and other spatial value/normal jumps unchanged. Then time-independent n(y) map [n_yy]=j of BR17 adds j A_na to mixed derivative jump, yielding conformal [A_new,y,na]=2j A_na. Tangential and normal already satisfy conformal condition (normal acceleration value zero). Shift velocity values and first spatial jets zero at seam; time-reflection parity g0i odd and g00,gij even. Set lapse second time jet to zero before spatial coordinate acceleration; induced g00,tt=-2N v.dN+2|v|² vanishes with first jets on seam. Thus for this constructed finite initial jet, g_t and g_tt have common values, lambda_t=lambda_tt=0 at seam (g0y,t and A_yy both zero there), and [g_y,t]=2j g_t, [g_y,tt]=2j g_tt. Equivalently the BR4 V_t,V_tt have common first jets, including the normal derivative. Initial H must now be recomputed from the contracted lower Christoffel of the corrected initial velocity, not blindly copy BR17 H_i=Gamma_i(gamma)-d_i log N; jump unchanged because added shift velocity has zero value/first jets at wall. Initial H_t chosen from contracted source of constructed acceleration, common remainder jet after subtracting -3j s(y)lambda dy; Hamiltonian/momentum constraints ensure C_t=0 as in BR17. This is a finite differentiated compatibility construction, not a proof all AO compatibility norms/orders hold, not a formal all-order convergence theorem, and not a solution of the independent wall equation at positive time. BQ fourth spatial jet retained for next higher time recursion; no new physical spatial modification. The coordinate acceleration is gauge freedom not a stationary shift solution.

## BR21–BR25 / BS9 — common variable principal energy, finite reduced existence, wall-force corner and full normal gauge form (27 November 2026)

Reconciliation: #1554 predecessor a4433a413b3b16e8c3697d22e326ee7d257301a4; audited #1547 actual head be40ae7db476263e7df0bab94ad59a3780ce710c (full relative-tensor bulk, retarded symmetrizer, material seam, root-adapted scalar preconditioner), #1291 c68b44f37c89b54d9ba0b5ef18ab4753c7dd5f04, #1508 5abb227a3c5733c243c7e2eabcba36e671b3e28a, #1512 52357be06c1ff086ad4c1c16d51852bcec122fec, #1527 d19a9525968ec2fee92a6e3f73027df05396d497. Inspected actual F8 check_f8_full_reduced_bulk.py, check_f8_retarded_reference_weld.py, check_f8_harmonic_material_seam.py, check_f8_damped_scalar_preconditioner.py and master KSW668F8FullCoupledWeightedSBPEnergyMaxCut20261115.md and KSW668F8PhysicalConstraintRadialSobolevMaxCut20261115.md. #1291 inventory reached 3000 paths; not an exhaustive search. Reuse their energy and signed operator transport, NOT F8's FRW matter, discrete radial commutation as continuum theorem, or its coordinate coefficients. BO8/BQ spatial construction and BR20 finite jets are the actual compatibility producers. No replacement spatial solver.

BR21. Variable-coefficient reduced metric energy in the BR4 fixed material chart. Write V=(bar h,beta,Q), g=(Q+p_cusp)^-2 bar g. Its common scalar wave principal a^{mu nu}=bar g^{mu nu} is a smooth algebraic function of bar h,beta, independent of Q and cusp. In particular a^{yy}=1 exactly, a^{ay}=-beta^a, a^{ab}=bar h^{ab}+beta^a beta^b. On a compact time chart with A=-a^{00}>0 and C=(a^{ij}) positive definite (true initially with zero shift and remains locally), B^i=a^{0i}. For one component u, w=u_t,d=grad_sp u, the first-order symmetrizer H=diag(1,A,C) makes all four principal evolution matrices symmetric: w_t=2B^i w_i/A+C^{ij}d_{j,i}/A+lower, d_t=grad w. Tensor with identity on all 15 packed components. This is the F8 moving-coordinate symmetrizer generalized to the actual arbitrary shift/variable principal metric of BR4; no assumption that second-order perturbations are bounded lower-order terms. For divergence equation partial_mu(a^{mu nu}partial_nu u)=F, exact local identity
 d_t e = partial_i(B^i w²+w C^{ij} d_j) - A_t w²/2 + w B_t.d + d^T C_t d/2 + u w - w F,
 e=(A w²+d^T C d+u²)/2.
 Signs follow the negative a^{00} convention. With all first jets common, both sides have identical normal flux and their signed contributions cancel, even with nonzero shift, time-variable coefficients and nonzero mean curvature. Inhomogeneous conormal residual r with common values and tangential jets contributes -w r in signed integrated flux, not a quantity bounded by bulk L² w alone. For homogeneous seam and controlled exterior work, if A,C coercivity and coefficient time derivatives bounded, obtain E'<=K E+K sqrt(E)||F||_2+outer work (take K from coefficient bounds and coercivity). This proves an actual compact-chart variable principal reduced bulk estimate, not a physical quotient energy or free-wall estimate.

BR22. Principal free-wall work identity and exact obstruction to a tempting separated positive energy. In BR9/18 adapted reference, use packed J=2j eta+6j nn, e_nn coordinate vector, m=mean p, d=mean p_y, r=[p_y], [p_t]=-J f_t (for general background additional lower-order material coefficients retained). For any real symmetric constant component multiplier W the total signed normal work is -[p_t^T W p_y]=-m_t^T W r+f_t J^T W d. The wall principal energy derivative from f_tt-Delta_h f+c f+mean p_nn,y/2=0 is -f_t e_nn^T d/2. To cancel the unrestricted mean conormal with a separated constant wall kinetic multiplier K requires WJ=K e_nn/2. Then J^T W J=4jK. Since j=-6/5<0, positive definite W forces K<0. At b=1 let normalized metric trace p=(H,beta,2l), proper/optical derivative p_x=(H_x,beta_x,2l_x), k_+=j/2,k_-=-j/2, z the tangential Laplace-Fourier covector, div beta=z^T eta beta. BR14 harmonic coordinate dy=dn/b^4, p normalized by b^2,b^5,b^8. BR26 block gives exact linearized physical rows in the fixed reference lower harmonic source:
 delta K=k H+(H_x-z tensor beta-beta tensor z)/2-k l eta;
 delta tau=tr_eta H_x/2-div beta-4k l;

 delta C_n=-tr_eta H_x/2+div beta+l_x.

The flat BR33 expression follows with k=0 and common H, flat normal transfer. The extension via mean H agrees with physical rows on continuity and preserves arbitrary forcing.

For the actual BR15 Liouville u=b^(R/2)p the signed physical derivative at each endpoint is p_x=u_x-k R p/2, no scalar substitution. R p has tangent 3H, mixed beta, normal 2tr H-5p_nn. For BS4/5 write C in orthonormal normal-parallel components v, with coordinate C_a=bwarp v_a and C_n=v_n. Covector optical u=bwarp^(3/2)v. In the frozen tangential RNC chart the exact coupled normal potential after Liouville is

BR33 coordinate Codazzi expression [partial_n C_a]+D_a[C_n]-2[K_a^b C_b] requires conversion: at reference partial_n C_a=v_x,a+k v_a, so actual parallel-component row is [v_x,a]+z_a[v_n]-(j/2)(v_+,a+v_-,a). General coordinate Codazzi retains -2 M eta [C]-j sum C with arbitrary mean M. No claim that AK frame formula holds in a general variable frame.

### BR35 analytic bounded-ratio cone inverse, not unrestricted glancing

Let L=|ell|, gamma=Re s>0, Z=max_a |z_a|; restrict a fixed K>=1 with Z/L<=K and L/|z0|<=K (second ratio only needed for E). Use BR15 |T|,|C| between 3L/4 and 4L/3 at sufficient damping. First impose homogeneous [H],[beta] (arbitrary prescribed values can be lifted and their actual boundary image subtracted). Norm X=L max(|H|_max,|beta+|_max,|beta-|_max,|l+|,|l-|), forcing Y maximum absolute derivative/force row component. The BR33 exact inverse gives X<=M_D(K)Y or M_E(K)Y:
 H0=2/3, P_D=4/3+4K, B_D=4(1+K P_D)/3, A_D=4 H0+4(4K B_D+4 P_D/3+2)/3,
 M_D=max(H0,B_D/2,(A_D+P_D)/2).
 P_E=4, B_s=4(1+4K)/3, B_E=max(B_s,K(3K B_s+1)), A_E=4H0+8/3,
 M_E=max(H0,B_E/2,(A_E+P_E)/2).
 Each follows directly by triangle inequality from forced formulas: |div v|<=4Z|v|, spatial contraction <=3Z|v|. D's q=-ell² and E's z0 divisions are not suppressed.

Define eps(gamma;c1,c0)=18(2c1/gamma+c0/gamma²)/(1-6(2c1/gamma+c0/gamma²)), provided denominator positive. BR15 gives endpoint derivative discrepancy in u <= eps L max endpoint p. Metric p endpoint norm <=2X/L and ||R||_infty=13, so normal derivative discrepancy <=(2 eps+13|k|/L)X. Physical Israel adds <=4|k|X/L, tangent gauge <=6|k|X/L, normal gauge derivative <=5/2 times derivative discrepancy, and mean force <=4 times derivative discrepancy+8|k|X/L. Thus all actual BR34 metric boundary residuals differ from the flat reduced rows by at most delta_m X with delta_m=8 eps(gamma;1536,30832/25)+60|k|/gamma. This bounds the actual full coupled transfer on the fixed reference cut. Mean force derivative must be included to get the 4 factor.

Gauge potential bound c1=8,c0=1239/100 follows from |bk|<=1, ||L_a||<=1, S norm4, b<=1, h0²=16/25. The same BR15 Dirichlet Green argument gives eps_g. On homogeneous gauge value rows (D: C_n^+=0,[C_a]=0; E: both C_n=0, spatial [C_a]=0), BR33 inverses with |T+j|,|T+2j|>=L/2 at gamma>=16|j| give M_gD=max(2,1+2K), M_gE=1+K/2 in X=L max endpoint covector amplitude, Y=max derivative row forcing. Actual BR34 parallel derivative error <=(eps_g+3|k|/(2L))X, tangent connection difference relative to flat BR33 <=|j|X/L. Hence delta_g=2 eps(gamma;8,1239/100)+5|k|/gamma bounds every derivative row discrepancy. Arbitrary value rows lifted before applying this argument.

If the relevant M delta<1/2, B_actual=B_flat(I+B_flat^-1 delta B) on the retained trace space has inverse by Neumann series with preconditioned inverse norm <=2, actual X<=2 M Y. For z=(s,i xi), ell=sqrt(s²+|xi|²), Re s>0, choose Re ell>0. Then Re ell>=Re s (from the real/imaginary root equations). Put a=s/ell, v=i xi/ell. The complex Lorentz matrix B=[[a,v^T],[v,I+vv^T/(1+a)]] satisfies B^T eta B=eta, B^-1=B(a,-v), B e0=z/ell. The chart is nonsingular because Re(s+ell)>0. No conjugation is used. Moreover |s+ell|>=|ell|: Im ell has the sign of Im s, so Re(s conjugate(ell))>=0. With Q=max(1,max|z_i|/|ell|), each boost entry is bounded by 1+Q², and the induced max row sum by 4(1+Q²); rank-two transport costs its square. These are polynomial frequency losses, not a uniform Kreiss estimate.

The BR14/15 frozen RNC tensor operators are built by metric contraction, normal insertion and the covector representation of tangential connection. D's physical rows have scalar, covector and symmetric-tensor types. The exact tensor transport therefore suggests reducing to z'=(ell,0,0,0), where BR35 K=1 applies with damping Re ell. For an invertible D, let x0=D^-1 f with released row set to zero, h=D^-1 e_r, g=actual new E row. Then x=x0+h(target-g x0)/(g h) solves E whenever g h is nonzero; det E/det D=g h up to permutation. Guard the actual denominator, not the flat comparison. In the flat BR33 model the respective response factors are -C z0/q and z0/(T+2j). A covariant scalar response to covector forcing is proportional to z0/ell times the rest-frame response; proving this via the full D intertwiner would allow E nonvanishing to follow from BR35 rest-frame E invertibility. Until that weld is checked, neither E nor D unrestricted-frequency acceptance is claimed.
